# Files under `src/` with a declaration under a `MAIN ...` banner that has no `/--` docstring.
#
# `check-conventions.sh` refuses any other file with one: a main declaration is what a reader
# meets first, and its docstring says in words what it states or computes. One path per line.
# The list only shrinks: give the file's main declarations docstrings and delete its line; an
# entry whose file has none left is reported as stale. Written 2026-10-15 from the tree as it
# stood; new files get their docstring at once.
